Saints seek enlightenment to escape from worldly suffering, but Mahasambhuti takes birth to take this suffering upon Himself. This is the night-and-day difference between the two. Baba revealed a poignant glimpse of the mystery of Mahasambhuti in one of His songs.

> ***HE VIRÁTA TAVA KATHÁ***
> ***AMITA SMITA VÁRTÁ***
> ***DURJJEYA ITIKATHÁ***
> ***NANDAN SPANDANE...***
> ***PRIITI SUDHÁRASE MÁKHI***
> ***VILÁYECHO NIJA PRÁŃE (4359)***
>
> **O Great Boundless One**
> **Your stories**
> **Your tremendous message**
> **Your intense smile**
> **Are immensely powerful.**
> **Their profound history**
> **Is full of joyous vibrations...**
> **By anointing (everyone) with**
> **Nectarean flows of love**
> **You have completely**
> **Dissolved and extinguished**
> **Your own life**
> **Your own Heart.**

# UNDERSTANDING KIIRTAN

In Latehar District (Nagpuria samaja) there was a small Amjharia Guesthouse amid the dense forests. The area is still a very poor area surrounded by tribals. There in 1970, two days before Diipavali, the margis and monks began singing the mahamantra Baba Nam Kevalam. Finally upon the blessed day of Diipavali, Baba sanctified this mantra with His acceptance.

What is kiirtana? Kiirtana, Baba says, literally means to sing the Lord's name loudly. Furthermore, kiirtan involves giving up all one's complexes, all one's social status by throwing up one's hands in the air and dancing together with everyone as part of one flow of love towards Him.

Then from January 1 to 3, 1971, Baba began a special form of kiirtan called Akhanda Kiirtan in the city of Patna. This kiirtan was done circling anti-clockwise around four photos of Baba. Counter-clockwise is the direction the kundalini moves up the spine. Hence the Sadguru has the disciple move around Him in an anti-clockwise manner. This is how in Vrindavan, Krsna made the gopis dance around Him while playing His flute. This kiirtan is supposed to be for a minimum of three hours. The hours could be increased by multiples of three — to six hours, nine hours and so on. Before Baba went to jail, we were graced during reporting with the opportunity to sing Akhanda Kiirtan where we would all dance around Baba. Circling around Baba as He radiated His bhávas (ecstasies) was an indescribable experience. The love that poured forth from Baba even when we are not able to feel His presence made us all dance like boats in a stormy ocean. Doing kiirtana like this around Baba for three hours automatically lifts one into the realm of divine love (prema).

### Avarta Kiirtana

Much later, after coming out of jail, Baba gave another form of kiirtana called Avarta Kiirtana. This is a special kiirtan where one starts doing kiirtan facing in one direction. Then one turns ninety degrees to the right and does kiirtan with another melody. Then one again turns ninety degrees to the right (the reverse of one's starting position) and sings another kiirtan melody. After this, one turns ninety degrees to the right (to the left of one's starting position) and sings a different kiirtan melody. Then one again turns ninety degrees to the right, coming back to the original position, but this time one sings another kiirtan facing upwards. Finally, one concludes with another kiirtana melody in the same direction but facing downwards. The special ideations Baba gave for this kiirtan are:

1. I am the embodiment of sincerity.
2. I have innate love for Parama Purusa.
3. I have purified my mind with the fire of devotion.
4. I am overcoming all internal and external obstacles.
5. O lord, take me onto Your lap.
6. I surrender my everything unto You.

During reporting and at other times in Lake Gardens, Ananda Nagar, RDS and at Tiljala, avarta kiirtan performed in front of Baba was an even more intense realization. Why do we move clockwise in avarta kiirtan? Because when a child makes the father dance around, they do so in a clockwise direction out of intense love. We did not feel we were surrounding Baba; rather we felt that in fact Baba was surrounding us and dedicating Himself with love to us — as if He was the lover and we were the beloved. Lord Krsna gave this realization to the gopis and Radha in His late childhood. The devotees pray for this realization because they know that it is when Baba surrounds the devotee that one has so much more chance to love Him. This is why it is said in one Prabhata Samgiita:

> **TUMI CHÁRÁ ÁMI NÁI JÁNE JAGATE**
> **TUMI MORE GHIRE ÁCHO CÁRI BHITE**
> **ÁNKHITE ASHRU NÁVE TAVA NÁMETE**
> **KÁCHE ESE MOR DOŚA TRUTI KŚAMA (4898)**
>
> **The whole universe knows that**
> **I have no existence without You.**
> **You please surround me from all the sides.**
> **By your name itself, tears come into my eyes.**
> **Please come very, very close,**
> **Forgive my defects, failures and mistakes.**

### Madhura sadhana

Madhura sadhana is a special form of avarta kiirtana that workers used to perform in front of Baba on special occasions in Bhaerava dress. It is to be done in this dress between 11:15 to 12:45 at night. Where there is no mental complex drawing the mind towards self-centredness, one begins to do kiirtan with intense bháva. And when so many devotees were there doing madhura kiirtan in front of Baba, how can that ecstasy be described? Even though Baba was sitting before us in silence, we would feel Him encircling us all the while we were singing to Him.

However in this kiirtan Radha and Krsna become one and the circling happens while merging together. But often we were not satisfied with this. We wanted Baba to be closer and closer while surrounding us. This blissful eagerness is revealed in one of His songs:

> **1668 (09/08/1984) D**
> **PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**
>
> **TOMÁR PARASH MADHUR SARAS**
> **PELAVATÁ DEY BHARE**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**

> **PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**
>
> **NANDAN VANE KENO TUMI EKÁ**
> **ÁÁÁÁÁÁÁÁÁ**
> **NANDAN VANE KENO TUMI EKÁ**
> **JYOTSNÁ NILAYE KENDE CALE RÁKÁ**
> **NANDAN VANE KENO TUMI EKÁ**
> **JYOTSNÁ NILAYE KENDE CALE RÁKÁ**
> **MANER GAHANE NIHSAUNGA KEKÁ**
> **KEHO NÁHI CÁRI DHÁRE**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**
>
> **PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**
>
> **ÁR KEHO NÁI MOR ÁPANÁR**
> **ÁÁÁÁÁÁÁÁÁ**
> **ÁR KEHO NÁI MOR ÁPANÁR**
> **VYATHÁR ASHRU MUCHÁTE ÁMÁR**
> **ÁR KEHO NÁI MOR ÁPANÁR**
> **VYATHÁR ASHRU MUCHÁTE ÁMÁR**
> **VISHVERA TUMI SÁRÁTSÁR**
> **SABE CÁHE TOMÁRE**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**
>
> **PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI, PRIYA TUMI**
> **THEKO ÁMÁRE GHIRE**

> **You my Darling, You my Beloved**
>
> **You my Sweetheart**
> **Please stay always encircling me!**
>
> **Your sweet and succulent touch**
> **Fills everything with tenderness.**
> **Please stay always encircling me!**
>
> **In this garden of joy,**
> **Why remain alone?**
>
> **The moonlight cries in my illuminated house.**
> **The peacock’s lonely cries**
>
> **Resound deep within my mind.**
>
> **Please stay always encircling me!**
>
> **No one else is there who is my very own dear**
>
> **Who can wipe away all my tears of agony.**
>
> **You are the quintessence of the universe.**
> **Everyone is yearning for You.**
>
> **Please stay always encircling me!**

This is why this madhura sadhana takes us to the threshold of mahábháva (where one feels His touch in every pore of one’s being and becomes lost in Him).

For those who were more ardent devotees, this bliss brought sorrows sweeter than the sweetest joys. Being so close to Baba and feeling Him all around but at the same time not being able to embrace Him to one’s heart’s content — this is the most precious agony. Why, you ask? Because it is such a privilege to experience this exquisite agony. Baba revealed the essence of this agony of separation in one song:

> **TUMI KÁCHE THEKEO KATA DÚRE**
> **DHARÁ NÁHI DÁO ÁMÁRE**
> **YADIO JÁNI ÁCHO GHIRE (2522)**
>
> **You are so close**
> **But at the same time**

> **You remain oh so very far**
> **You never come into my embrace**
> **Even though I know full well**
> **How You are encircling and surrounding me.**

Today those blissful days seem so far away, like in another lifetime. We have forgotten what it was like when Baba's love would pour out of His physical form, filling our hearts and making them pour love back unto Him. Today we just sadly sing:

> ***TOMÁRE CÁI JE EKÁNTE***
> ***HE ANANTA***
> ***JÁNI MORE GHIRE ÁCHO CÁRI DHÁRE***
> ***TABU KHUNJE KHUNJE PARISHRÁNTA* (1027)**
>
> **In lonely solitude**
> **O endless Beloved,**
> **O how I yearn for You**
> **I know You are encircling me**
> **Surrounding me in all directions;**
> **But still, I become exhausted**
> **Searching and searching for You.**

# PRESERVING PRECIOUS PEARLS

I have mentioned before about the great Asiimanandji. His greatness was multidimensional: as the overseer of countless plants in Baba's Garden, as the recorder of Baba's knowledge on agriculture, and as one whose excellence in sadhana and good conduct was exceeded only by his humility and gentle demeanour.

On April 2, 1990, Asiimanandji was brutally murdered by communists in Ananda Nagar. This was part of a wave of attacks on Ananda Marga in which countless buildings were damaged or destroyed. Asiimanandji's body was brought to Kolkata. Baba wanted to see the body. When He saw Dadaji's mutilated body, Baba said that the greatest agricultural scientist in the world had been killed.

In that atmosphere of intense sorrow, some didis who were present started weeping.

Baba asked sternly, "Who is weeping?"

Then Baba said, "No, keep these tears in your eyes. They are so precious, so do not shed them."

Sannyasis are not supposed to weep, especially not for their own pains and sorrows. They are to do sadhana and service to transcend their personal pains. But to feel anguish at the suffering of others is also the sign of a true sannyasi.

As Baba said:

> **Only those sannyásiis whose minds are deeply pained at the gloomy touch of human sorrow — whose minds sparkle like rubies and emeralds with the tears of joy and laughter of all beings — find success in their vow of renunciation and in their spiritual life. (Discourse 11, *Namah Shiváya Shántáya*)**

### Infinite Indebtedness

Many times in the final days in 1990, dadas saw how Baba's health was declining. Both Sarvatmananda and Vijayananda requested Baba to teach them how to initiate people into kapalika sadhana because during kapalika sadhana initiation, Baba used to take on large amounts of samskaras and this told severely on His health. Baba, however, refused, saying that during kapalika sadhana initiation He would strengthen the disciples' ajina cakra and these dadas did not have the power to do this.

A few days before Baba's public physical departure on October 1990, Baba was given the list of a large number of possible candidates for avadhuta and avadhutika diksa. He had asked the dadas to recommend as many names as possible. Baba approved every single candidate, including those who had been rejected many times. This surprised the dadas greatly since normally Baba would usually only approve some of the names. This time however Baba approved seventy-eight candidates for kapalika initiation. Then Baba asked if there was anyone left. Then Baba was told that one candidate had been withheld because he had been rejected by the dharma pracar secretary, the global training secretary and his departmental secretary. They all felt that his work and his conduct did not merit initiation into kapalika sadhana. However, Baba called this dada also for initiation.

After initiating all seventy-nine candidates, Baba called this dada who had been blacklisted for his poor performance and substandard conduct. Baba said, "My little boy, everyone in the organization rejected you. But I called you for initiation. For this what are you going to give Me?"

The boy replied, "Baba, what else can I give? I can only give you thanks."

"Only thanks? That is all?"

Then Baba said, "You will not be able to repay the debt of this grace even if you sacrifice one hundred of your lives for Me."

# BABA AS HE IS

> **If you want mukti (liberation) or mokśa (non-qualified liberation) from Him, if you are a suitable candidate then you may get it from Him, but you will not get Him, because you did not want Him. So an intelligent spiritual aspirant will say: "I want You, I do not want anybody else. And why do I want You? Not because Your presence will give me pleasure, but because Your presence will give me the scope to serve You, because Your presence will give the chance to give You pleasure — not to give pleasure to myself." ("Kŕśńa, the Nucleus of the Universe," *Ánanda Vacanámrtam Part 2*)**

Who really wants Taraka Brahma? The people of this planet have never accepted Lord Shiva (the first Mahasambhuti), neither in philosophy nor in sadhana as Taraka Brahma. The people still accepted Him as God who lives in heaven or on a holy mountain and who is easily pleased. This is why the names Ashutosh or Bhole Baba'. They do not like however Shiva's vajra (thunderous weapon) or the play of His trident. Shiva has not been accepted as the beloved Lord present in every moment of our daily lives. His countless contributions to human civilization have been forgotten and His benevolence in granting these is completely unappreciated. The reality is that whatever civilization or culture humanity on this planet has, Lord Shiva laid the foundations.

As said earlier, Baba maintained that the tandava given by Shiva should be performed collectively in public. The reason is that when the manliness is lost and does not protect humanity or dharma (righteousness), at that time the performance of tandava in public will arouse the people to speak out and fight against injustice. Let us be clear that by manliness we mean undaunted courage,

radiant energy and the confidence to leap into battle (totally outnumbered), laughing all the while and terrifying the anti-social elements. Let us be clear that Tantra may have been born in Rarh (western Bengal) but it has spread all over the world. The berzerker of Scandinavia is just one example of the evolution of the Tantric cult of the spiritual warrior. Let us furthermore be clear that this path of the spiritual warrior was one for both women and men. None of Shiva’s disciples was as great a warrior or as great in meditation as Kali, His wife.

Since then, whenever Baba arrived or departed from a function like DMC or DMS (Dharma Maha Sammelan or great assembly of righteous spiritualists), we did tandava in public. This practice started from the DMC in Gazipur in 1965-66 when the tandava with skull and dagger was done in a procession through the city. Baba made carrying the trident a part of the uniform of His avadhutas in the early days.

Tandava is not only a yogic or spiritual exercise; it also has a social purpose. It awakens and increases one’s manliness so that a human being will remain ready at all times to fight against injustice, inequality and exploitation.

Morality is the base, humanity is the means and life divine is the goal. Morality and humanity are closely related to each other. The degree of morality is judged on the basis of the humanity observed in one’s daily conduct.

What is humanity? To whatever extent a person has sacrificed his or her comfort, physical or mental, to provide comfort to others; to whatever degree such a sadhaka radiates selfless love to everyone — this represents their humanity. And if one sacrifices with the ideation of loving the Supreme Consciousness, this is known as divinity and the process of attaining divinity is sadhana.

Shiva as the first Mahasambhuti radiated the energy of Taraka Brahma in sadhana, in individual life and in collective life. He laid down the moral, social, cultural and spiritual foundations of human civilization. His greatest contribution was the introduction of the system of marriage based on equality and a mission in life of spiritual meditation and the spread of spiritual culture based on social equality. With the start of a spiritual family, the seed of the future cosmic family was sown. All of Shiva’s teachings are lost and today no one even realizes that He is a historical figure, let alone what His contributions were to human civilization. Only a Mahasambhuti can fully reveal the glory of another Mahasambhuti. Thus the third Mahasambhuti has revealed the glory of the life and ideology of Shiva, the first Mahasambhuti, in His discourses, such as "Tantra and Indo-Aryan Civilization" and "Bhaerava and Bhaeravii," in His book *Namah Shivaya Shantaya* and in His songs (Prabhata Samgiita).

The contributions of Krsna, the second Mahasambhuti, are no less significant than those of Shiva. Krsna’s role as Krsna of Vrndavan is not well known; people have failed to comprehend its actual significance. How He played the flute and awakened the kundalini and devotion in disciples and made the whole universe vibrate and dance with bliss and love is unknown. Baba explained the science of how Krsna attracts the devotee, the science of overwhelming charm (*sammohana vijinana*) or supra-aesthetic science.

From the age of eighteen when Krsna left Vrndavan until the time of the Mahabharata war when He was in His sixties, what did He do? Not much has been recorded. His mission as Mahasambhuti is a mystery. It was Baba in His book *Namami Krsna Sundaram* who revealed His role in arousing the social consciousness of the common people to struggle against injustice and to dream and fight for dharmarajya, a society ruled by righteousness. While Shiva spread the glory of Taraka Brahma on the physical level, it was Krsna who spread the glory of Taraka Brahma on the psychic level in the search for the noble ideals of dharmarajya. Humanity was further enriched by the foundation of love and devotion at the collective level. It was the heartache (*viraha*) of this love that created the environment for the revolutionary transformation of the Pandavas into spiritual warriors.

When humanity on this planet was facing unparalleled barbarism in the form of the First World War; the horrific Russian Civil War between the fascists and the communists; when countless people in Africa, Asia and South America suffered under the exploitation and crimes of white colonialism; when contemporary religious violence was beginning to unfold — at this time the third Mahasambhuti, Anandamurti, took birth. Universal Humanity as a collective entity needs to be established and all opposing forces such as national-

ism, religion, race and speciesism (human violence against nature) must be defeated. Humanity has to be sublimated into universal humanity.

While human beings can legitimately claim to have knowledge of some aspects of the material world, they have not yet begun to understand the existence of Taraka Brahma, let alone understand His role as Mahasambhuti. To acquire knowledge of samadhi, cosmology, microvita, cakras is relatively easy, but to have real knowledge of Baba as Taraka Brahma is very difficult indeed. Taraka Brahma has never been accepted in individual or collective life — neither in philosophy nor in practice. This is the reason why India, though it has such powerful spiritual vibrations from Shiva, Krsna and Anandamurti, has failed to understand their significance. Thus Indian society is debased by the violence of casteism, religious discrimination and so much exploitation and inhumanity. Although Shiva, Krsna and Anandamurti created equality for women and gave them dignity, people have refused to understand and practice it.

While Krsna worked on the psychic level but was only understood on the physical level, Baba worked on the spiritual level but was only understood on the physical and psychic levels to a small extent. It is Baba who is establishing the glory of Taraka Brahma in countless ways on this planet. We only get occasional glimpses of His cosmic mission and His cosmic family.

Baba as Parama Purusa wants devotion to become a vast, tremendous flow in human society like the river Ganges. Baba said in jail that He did not come here to establish sadvipra samaj (society of spiritual revolutionaries) — that is only a fraction of His true purpose. As He said, His real mission is to flood the world with devotion.

Above all, we cannot really understand His sacrifice because He always hid it behind His dazzling smile. Thus far, while people meditate on the liilas of Shiva and Krsna, very few have understood or meditated on the liila of Anandamurti on this planet.

Once, when Baba came to know that some workers were planning to approach the Nobel Foundation to recommend Baba’s name for a Nobel Prize, He became very angry. He said, “Who has the power and authority to accord Me any prize!” Baba explained that He loves His children and for their welfare He gives everything. Then Baba said, “Humanity is the highest truth on this earth. Observe it.”

I could feel the power of this statement, coming from Him, the embodiment of the perfection of humanity — the newly explained humanity of His neohumanism. But these feelings about Baba's true stance and role may come and go and that is why, due to our lack of deep understanding of Baba, we make mistakes.

Yet despite this lack of understanding, we all know how Baba takes care of us. Every problem, every crisis, every attack on us is most precious because then Baba is forced to openly show us that He is still there on this planet to save His devotee. So many stories can be told. Maybe for some, they are too simple but for me they are precious. And one such tiny gem is as follows: One story that comes to me is regarding the. In 1967, the village committee in the village of Chaparai in Etah District of Braja Samaja (UP) donated more than 125 acres of barren land. One school, one children’s home and a jagriti were constructed. A small portion of the land was used for growing rice and wheat. During the Emergency, the government gave the land to people of the untouchable castes. When the Emergency was lifted, Ananda Marga claimed the property and the issue went to the court. Since ‘possession is nine-tenths of the law’, the situation became volatile.

In February 2002, they started ploughing the land just to show that they were in possession. At that time I was the global master unit secretary and so I rushed there with ten dadas and stayed in the jagriti. They had planned to attack the ashram just before sunrise with axes, lathis and spades. In order to protect our jagriti and land, we decided to go out to meet them. Out of regard for my age the other dadas told me not to join them in the front, in case a clash took place.

Our dadas were dragged to the road and beaten. I noticed that four dadas, Niitishivananda, Phanishananda, Sanjivananda and Devaprakashananda had been struck on the head and appeared to be seriously injured. Suddenly I felt as if an invisible force were pushing me to go to their aid. I rushed out of the building to the road. Hardly had I passed the boundary when I fell down and my left leg was broken. This was very strange since there was no ditch to trip me nor was anyone nearby. One old man shouted, “Old dada has

fallen. Let us leave this place and go back home.” Thus the lives of the dadas were saved. Had I not fallen and broken my leg, the two dadas would have been killed.

I clearly felt that it was Baba who had pushed me out of the building and it was He who caused my leg to be broken. I had been feeling that Baba was cleaning my mind and body with external punishment (physical and verbal) every month at RDS. I felt that I needed His punishment for some mistakes I had been making. I was sure that Baba had arranged for my punishment by breaking my leg, thus saving the lives of these dadas. I felt Baba’s presence so strongly at that time, that when I was carried to a margi’s house I gave him money to buy sweets, which I distributed to everyone present. I was happy to have received punishment from Baba.

But despite all of this we have not yet begun to really appreciate all the love He pours down on us all the time and how He saves us from daily crises. As Baba said, Taraka Brahma can be understood only by the highest devotion. This is why I can never forget Baba’s call:

> **Therefore, O devotees remember the Lord’s Name else all your efforts will be reduced to cipher (zero). Under all circumstances and during all activities, cling firmly to His name. (Bhaktitattva)**

***

# BABA TODAY

> ***HÁRIYECHI JE DIN GULI,***
> ***TÁRÁO TOMÁR CARAN’ DHÚLI***
> ***TOMÁR SÁTHE ÁCHE MISHE,***
> ***CIRA KÁLER PARIPREKS’ITE (1361)***

> **Immersed in those lost days,**
> **I merge with the dust of Your feet,**
> **Endlessly reliving them for eternity.**

This verse from Prabhat Samgiit is our life today. But we are not just living in the beauty of His love in the past or in the crises of the present — we are surviving only because His love protects us today. In so many ways, in so many small incidents through which He helps you and me, Baba proves the immortality of His love. And this love is not just for old disciples, but for suffering beings everywhere upon this planet. One such story of His aid is unforgettable.

I went to Iceland in November 2011. I hadn’t expected it would be so cold. Acarya Ambareshvarananda had organized my tour and I supposed to stay in Iceland for a month. I was very excited. Baba had visited Iceland and Ananda Marga had its own jagriti and Didi's school there. November 1 was the first night for my kapalika sadhana. The weather forecast had predicted a very dry day.

When my plane landed at the airport, it started snowing and within a short time the snow covered the whole of Reykjavik. I thanked Shrii Gurumurtiji, the president of AMPS Iceland, for coming to receive me. We reached our jagriti where a few margi brothers and sisters and Didi Ananda Kaostubha were waiting. We all had a good time and had our dinner together. Around, 9 p.m. all left for home. Shrii Gurumurti told me that since that night was the first night for kapalika sadhana, he would take me to the cemetery for sadhana the next night.

After 10:30, I received a call from Didi Ananda Kaostubha that the next night and the following one would have heavy snowfall and the weather would be windy. She advised me that I should instead go out at that moment and survey the surrounding area for a place to do my night sadhana.

I went out and surveyed the area and made up my mind to do sadhana in a lonely, secluded space under the open sky. It was severe, bone-biting cold and there was snow all around. But I was happy thinking of performing my sadhana after midnight.

When I came back to enter the jagriti, I found the door locked. I tried to open it in but failed. How it had happened, I could not understand. While going out, I had just closed the door after me; now I found out that it was locked. I realized that I had not taken the key with me. I was not familiar with that type of lock. I began abusing myself for this mistake and needlessly putting myself in harm's way. Then I started remembering Baba with complete surrender. It was cold and not a soul was to be seen. I surveyed the building, searching for some way of getting in but there was no way in.

I had not taken much warm clothes with me, thinking that I would be back indoors soon, so I was shivering with the cold. Realizing that I might freeze, I went to the road some 150 metres from the jagriti. I thought I would die. A car appeared on the road and I signaled to it. The driver opened his car and said, "Please come inside. You are shivering." Upon my request, he brought me to the jagriti. He called a locksmith to open the door but they refused to come at such a late hour.

Then I said that I could break the glass windows and enter. To my shock, he said that if I did so, he would call the police and have me arrested. Even if it was my home, I was not allowed to do this. I was happy to hear this, because it showed the people's spirit of strict adherence to good conduct. Later I came to know that it is the only country in the world that has no military.

He suggested that I accompany him to his house and spend the night there. I was surprised. A person who didn't know me wanted to take me to his house. I was so happy to see what a good person he was.

Hardly had I gotten inside his car, when another car entered the parking area. It was Gurumurti. I was very happy indeed. Gurumurti said, "Dada, when I was sleeping, my wife Ranjita jolted me three times and said, 'Go to the jagriti. Dada is in trouble. If you don't go, then I will go.' She said that Baba had shown her that dada was in trouble. So I was forced to come. Now I see that you are actually in trouble, just like she said."

They had been married as per the Ananda Marga system and Ranjita was working in Didi's school. Gurumurti told me that the next two nights would be windy and so he took me to the cemetery so that I could do proper night sadhana. It was a wonderful night sadhana. I was thanking Him so much, feeling His love so strongly, just like I had all the years before 1990.

That love is revealed not just when He saves us. That love is fragrant in the dust of His feet in the form of His countless gifts to humanity. Every dust particle of His feet is a wondrous jewel that will radiate inspiration, enlightenment and the courage to fight uncompromisingly against injustice. We have so many ideas about service, sadhana, devotion, diet, microvita and so much more. However these are not merely intellectual concepts; they are glowing with spiritual energy and when we surrender to Him, these ideas, like stars, illumine the dark sky of our minds. And when we bring this light into the collective mind, into society, this is the ideological life. In June 1978, while in jail, Baba gave a special revelation of His ideology and mission:

> **Unlike other gurus, He (Baba) has no bows and arrows, no trishula — nothing of the kind. His all-vibrating philosophy combined with all-embracing spirit and strict sense of discipline takes the shape of His sudarshana cakra (divine weapon against immoral forces). He operates this cakra with the help of His sons and daughters.**
>
> **It is desirable that His sons and daughters acquire the inner strength to help Him in this respect. Strict adherence to Sixteen Points will give them that inner strength.**

Thus we understand that Baba's weapon and His real mission exist beyond the physical world. When communism fell in Europe, Baba

said it was because of PROUT. This confused many people, because on the physical level PROUT was barely present in the Soviet Bloc. What they failed to realize is that it was Baba's weapon, or sudarshana cakra, that destroyed communism in the higher realms of the collective psychology and this is what led to the collapse of communism. Today His weapon is destroying capitalism.

After the demise of communism, mushrooms of religious dogma and hatred arose and spread, and now that capitalism also is dying, these religions are showing their inner poisons. It is the Aryan religions with violent scriptures that have killed so many innocent people in the Americas, Middle East (Southwest Asia), Africa, India and Asia. These imperialistic religions are now destroying this planet. They pose the greatest danger to humanity. Baba predicted that they would fight each other and He warned us not to take sides. They will destroy each other. In this conflict Baba's divine grace will bless humanity with the blissful vision (sudarshana) of the creation as one Supreme Consciousness.

In *Shabda Cayanika Parts 4-5*, Baba explained who is a bhairava. It is not the statue I saw as a boy. Baba explained that Shiva used to radiate tremendous spiritual vibrations through His music and His spiritual blessings. Those disciples who became strong due to complete surrender to Shiva developed the capacity to broadcast those spiritual vibrations where they were living. Baba once explained that to create these kinds of vibrations in different places was the work He gave His first bhairavas like Kalicharan. Unlike Shiva, Baba has in addition also released tremendous vibrations in the psychic sphere in the form of His ideology. These vibrations in the psychic sphere are transforming and revolutionizing the collective psychology. They will bring sadvipra samaja and Ananda Parivara (Blissful Cosmic Family) on this planet and on other planets. In a discourse published in the book *Birds and Animals: Our Neighbours*, Baba explained that the next stage in human evolution is the transformation of the *buddha*, or enlightened sage propagating morality and compassion, into the *kalki*, the devotional warrior against injustice who travels from planet to planet, to the farthest limits of the universe, spreading universal love and equality (sama-samaja tattva) among all members of our cosmic family. As Baba said in Jamaica:

> **So many animate objects, so many inanimate objects are there, but they are all within His mind and not outside. A man may be educated, may be illiterate, may be of fair complexion, may be of dark complexion, may be poor or may be rich, but all are within His mind. All are His psychic creation, all are His loving children, loving progeny of the loving Progenitor. So nobody is unimportant, nobody is insignificant. Nobody should be suppressed, oppressed or repressed. Now these created beings, they being the progeny of the same Father are members of the same Universal Family. They have got a common tie of the Universal Fraternity (brotherhood) and Sorority (sisterhood). We must not forget this Supreme Fact. (Causal Matrix)**

This is the last time that Baba will come to this planet, and so this time His mission will become manifest. Shiva was born in a prehistoric era when society was in transition from the Shudra Era of unthinking people dominated by their environment to the Ksatriya Era of dynamic, courageous and skilled warriors who dominated their environment by their physical strength and courage. Society was moving from an individualistic lifestyle to a group lifestyle. At that time, people thought that numbers were everything and that if one had a big group then one could establish one's dominance. Shiva showed that by intense Tantra sadhana an ordinary person could become a godlike being who could fight against many people and could inspire a small group of people to resist and defeat a much larger army. This is how Shiva established the glory of the spiritual warrior.

Krsna was also born during a time of transition in human history when society was moving from the Ksatriya Era of warriors and kings to the Vipra Era of intellectuals who through their discoveries and ideals created a subtler human civilization. At that time people believed only in military power. Krsna gave Duryodhana, the immoral king, a choice between Krsna and his army. However Krsna said that He personally would remain unarmed and would not fight. Duryodhana would have chosen Krsna, since he knew Krsna's greatness as a warrior, but when he was told that Krsna would not fight, he chose Krsna's army because he was sure that

without fighting no one could win a war. Krsna showed that through the use of benevolent intellect He could establish His mission of arousing social consciousness to unite the various kingdoms and create a national community based on mutual love and sacrifice. Krsna also showed how by dedicating oneself to karma yoga, or meditation in action, an ordinary warrior could become a wise saint and establish the rule of righteousness (Dharmarajya). This is how Krsna established the glory of the spiritual intellectual.

Baba was born at a much more important transition at the time of humanity's quest for transcendence from this endless cycle that has led to the rise and fall of so many civilizations to the creation of a new state of existence that Baba termed Cosmic Humanity, transcending first the material realm and then the psychic realm. Thus far the collective mind of various societies has not developed the higher or superconscious mind. As a result, the collective mind has been manipulated and degraded by the environment and by exploiters. Baba is creating a revolution in the collective mind that will lead the human beings of this entire planet to develop a collective superconscious mind. This will revolutionize humanity and transform it into divinity. Baba revealed this glorious future in the third to the last DMC at Ananda Nagar:

> **So in this phase of introversial movement, when crude physicality is being transmuted into psychic, we can expect that the day is sure to come when the whole world will move from the subtle psychic realm and cross the threshold of the still more subtle spiritual world. And that day when the entire living world — dashing through a transitory phase of psychic — will become spiritual will not be in the distant future. (Biological Transformation Associated with Psychic Metamorphosis and Vice Versa)**

Today also human beings are bound by their faith in physicality. Many accept that a great saint can revolutionize a society but they still believe that without a physical body no one can transform society. They feel that without the physical and intellectual force of a body, no one can transform this world. This is certainly true for a non-devotee but for the Lord it is not just possible but inevitable.

One arrogant communist even said that power flows out of the barrel of a gun. Other arrogant people feel that by the power of their malevolent intellect to create dangerous weapons and diseases they can control the world. Baba however has clearly said:

> **The mutative force is a hundred thousand times more powerful than the static force, and the sentient force is a hundred thousand times more powerful than the mutative force; hence you should not be afraid of any force in the universe. ("Miscellaneous," *Caryacarya Part 2*)**

Baba did not just teach this; today He is proving this as He guides humanity towards its destiny. Once in the early 1980s during the winter DMC, Baba asked the devotees what was the greatest tragedy of the Mahabharata war. People thought many things but no one could give a definite answer. Finally Baba said that the greatest tragedy of the Mahabharata was that Krsna had to take part and had to see all His children dying. Baba then declared that He would not make the same mistake.

And so today many people, including the capitalist exploiters, believe that Baba is gone. But as anyone who does sadhana properly knows, as every devotee feels in every breath they take, Baba is not just alive but so intensely real that He alone is the reality of this universe. He is not in heaven, He is right here with you as you are reading this book. Baba gave us the gift of heartache of not having easy access to His physical darshan. Heartache is the greatest blessing.

And this all starts with remembering and practicing Baba's most precious vanii:

> **Utilizing the vibrations created for eternal time**
> **by Shrii Shrii Ánandamúrtijii through Jánusparsha**
> **and Varábhaya Mudrás, take**
> **yourself and the entire universe**
> **ahead along the path of**
> **all-round welfare.**
> **Oḿ Shánti!**
